They may be updated, modified, or replaced by their original maintainers at any time after being added here.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBefore installing or using any Agent Skill, review potential security risks and validate the source yourself.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRecommended tools:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/snyk/agent-scan\"\u003eSynk Skill Security Scanner\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://ai.gendigital.com/agent-trust-hub\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eAgent Trust Hub\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAgent skills can include prompt injections, tool poisoning, hidden malware payloads, or unsafe data handling patterns. Always review the code and use skills at your own discretion.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" The following references and criteria keep the bar high.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eQuality Criteria\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-quality-criteria\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Quality Criteria\" href=\"#quality-criteria\"\u003e\u003csvg class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eArea\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eGuideline\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDescription\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWrite in third person. State \u003cem\u003ewhat\u003c/em\u003e the skill does and \u003cem\u003ewhen\u003c/em\u003e to use it. Use specific keywords agents can match on (e.g., \"PostgreSQL migration\" not \"database stuff\").\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eProgressive disclosure\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eKeep top-level metadata under ~100 tokens. Skill body should stay below 500 lines. Load resources (large docs, schemas) on demand, not inline.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNo absolute paths\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNever hard-code machine-specific paths like \u003ccode\u003e/Users/alice/\u003c/code\u003e. Use relative paths or well-known variables (\u003ccode\u003e$HOME\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e$PROJECT_ROOT\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eScoped tools\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRequest only the tools the skill actually needs. Avoid blanket \u003ccode\u003e\"tools\": [\"*\"]\u003c/code\u003e. Declare tool dependencies explicitly.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e🤝 Contributing\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content--contributing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 🤝 Contributing\" href=\"#-contributing\"\u003e\u003csvg class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWe welcome contributions! See \u003ca href=\"/VoltAgent/awesome-agent-skills/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md\"\u003eCONTRIBUTING.md\u003c/a\u003e for guidelines.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSubmit new skills via PR\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eImprove existing definitions\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNote:\u003c/strong\u003e Please don't submit skills you created 3 hours ago. We're now focusing on community-adopted skills, especially those published by development teams and proven in real-world usage. Quality over quantity.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eContributor \u003cg-emoji <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Awesome Agent Skills</h1><a id="user-content-awesome-agent-skills" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Awesome Agent Skills" href="#awesome-agent-skills"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Unlike many bulk-generated skill repositories, this collection focuses on real-world Agent Skills created and used by actual engineering teams, not mass AI‑generated stuff.</p>
<p dir="auto">This collection features official skills published by leading development teams, including Anthropic, Google Labs, Vercel, Stripe, Cloudflare, Netlify, Trail of Bits, Sentry, Expo, Hugging Face, Figma, and more, alongside community-built skills.</p>
<p dir="auto">Compatible with Claude Code, Codex, Antigravity, Gemini CLI, Cursor, GitHub Copilot, OpenCode, Windsurf, and more. See the table below for paths and documentation.</p>
<p dir="auto">The most contributed Agent Skills repository, built and maintained together with the community.</p>

<summary><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Product Manager Skills by Dean Peters</h3><a id="user-content-product-manager-skills-by-dean-peters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Product Manager Skills by Dean Peters" href="#product-manager-skills-by-dean-peters"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div></summary>
<p dir="auto">46 battle-tested product management skills by <a href="https://github.com/deanpeters">Dean Peters</a>. Frame problems, hunt opportunities, scaffold validation experiments, and kill bad bets fast — with frameworks from Teresa Torres, Geoffrey Moore, Amazon, MITRE, and more.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Component Skills</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/acquisition-channel-advisor">deanpeters/acquisition-channel-advisor</a></strong> - Evaluate channels using unit economics and recommend scale/test/kill decisions</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/ai-shaped-readiness-advisor">deanpeters/ai-shaped-readiness-advisor</a></strong> - Assess automation vs. redesign opportunities across five competencies</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/altitude-horizon-framework">deanpeters/altitude-horizon-framework</a></strong> - Navigate the PM→Director mindset shift covering scope, time horizons, and failure modes</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/business-health-diagnostic">deanpeters/business-health-diagnostic</a></strong> - Diagnose SaaS health, identify red flags, and prioritize recovery actions</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/company-research">deanpeters/company-research</a></strong> - Deep-dive competitor or company analysis</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/customer-journey-map">deanpeters/customer-journey-map</a></strong> - Map customer experience across touchpoints using the NNGroup framework</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/eol-message">deanpeters/eol-message</a></strong> - Communicate product or feature deprecation gracefully</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/epic-hypothesis">deanpeters/epic-hypothesis</a></strong> - Turn initiatives into testable hypotheses with measurable success metrics</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/finance-metrics-quickref">deanpeters/finance-metrics-quickref</a></strong> - Reference guide for 32+ SaaS finance metrics with formulas and benchmarks</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/jobs-to-be-done">deanpeters/jobs-to-be-done</a></strong> - Understand customer objectives using the JTBD framework</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/pestel-analysis">deanpeters/pestel-analysis</a></strong> - Analyze external factors across Political, Economic, Social, Tech, Environmental, and Legal dimensions</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/pol-probe">deanpeters/pol-probe</a></strong> - Define lightweight validation experiments to test hypotheses</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/positioning-statement">deanpeters/positioning-statement</a></strong> - Define target audience, problem solved, and differentiation using Geoffrey Moore's framework</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/press-release">deanpeters/press-release</a></strong> - Clarify product vision with a future press release using Amazon's Working Backwards method</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/problem-statement">deanpeters/problem-statement</a></strong> - Frame customer problems with evidence before jumping to solutions</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/proto-persona">deanpeters/proto-persona</a></strong> - Create hypothesis-driven personas before conducting full research</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/recommendation-canvas">deanpeters/recommendation-canvas</a></strong> - Document AI-powered product recommendations</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/saas-economics-efficiency-metrics">deanpeters/saas-economics-efficiency-metrics</a></strong> - Calculate unit economics and capital efficiency including CAC, LTV, payback, and Rule of 40</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/saas-revenue-growth-metrics">deanpeters/saas-revenue-growth-metrics</a></strong> - Track revenue, retention, and growth metrics including MRR/ARR, churn, NRR, and expansion</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/storyboard">deanpeters/storyboard</a></strong> - Visualize user journeys with 6-frame narrative storyboards</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/user-story">deanpeters/user-story</a></strong> - Write user stories with acceptance criteria using Mike Cohn and Gherkin formats</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/user-story-mapping">deanpeters/user-story-mapping</a></strong> - Organize stories by user workflow using Jeff Patton's story mapping approach</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/user-story-splitting">deanpeters/user-story-splitting</a></strong> - Break down large stories using 8 proven splitting patterns</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Interactive Skills</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/context-engineering-advisor">deanpeters/context-engineering-advisor</a></strong> - Diagnose context stuffing vs. engineering and guide memory and retrieval design</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/customer-journey-mapping-workshop">deanpeters/customer-journey-mapping-workshop</a></strong> - Guide journey mapping sessions with pain point identification</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/director-readiness-advisor">deanpeters/director-readiness-advisor</a></strong> - Coach the PM→Director transition across four key situations</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/discovery-interview-prep">deanpeters/discovery-interview-prep</a></strong> - Plan customer interviews using Mom Test style based on research goals</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/epic-breakdown-advisor">deanpeters/epic-breakdown-advisor</a></strong> - Split epics into stories using Richard Lawrence's 9 splitting patterns</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/feature-investment-advisor">deanpeters/feature-investment-advisor</a></strong> - Evaluate features using ROI and strategic value scoring</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/finance-based-pricing-advisor">deanpeters/finance-based-pricing-advisor</a></strong> - Evaluate pricing changes using financial impact analysis</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/lean-ux-canvas">deanpeters/lean-ux-canvas</a></strong> - Set up hypothesis-driven planning using Jeff Gothelf's Lean UX Canvas v2</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/opportunity-solution-tree">deanpeters/opportunity-solution-tree</a></strong> - Generate opportunities and solutions and recommend proof-of-concept tests</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/pol-probe-advisor">deanpeters/pol-probe-advisor</a></strong> - Recommend prototype type: Feasibility, Task-Focused, Narrative, Synthetic, or Vibe</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/positioning-workshop">deanpeters/positioning-workshop</a></strong> - Guide positioning definition with adaptive discovery questions</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/prioritization-advisor">deanpeters/prioritization-advisor</a></strong> - Recommend the right prioritization framework (RICE, ICE, Kano, etc.) for your situation</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/problem-framing-canvas">deanpeters/problem-framing-canvas</a></strong> - Lead through MITRE Problem Framing: Look Inward, Outward, and Reframe</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/tam-sam-som-calculator">deanpeters/tam-sam-som-calculator</a></strong> - Project market size with real-world data and citations</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/user-story-mapping-workshop">deanpeters/user-story-mapping-workshop</a></strong> - Walk through creating story maps with backbone and release slices</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/vp-cpo-readiness-advisor">deanpeters/vp-cpo-readiness-advisor</a></strong> - Coach the Director→VP/CPO transition including a CEO interview framework</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/workshop-facilitation">deanpeters/workshop-facilitation</a></strong> - Add step-by-step facilitation with numbered recommendations to any workshop</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Workflow Skills</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/discovery-process">deanpeters/discovery-process</a></strong> - Full discovery cycle: frame problem → research → synthesize → validate (3-4 weeks)</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/executive-onboarding-playbook">deanpeters/executive-onboarding-playbook</a></strong> - 30-60-90 day diagnostic playbook for VP/CPO onboarding transitions</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/prd-development">deanpeters/prd-development</a></strong> - Structured PRD process: problem → personas → solution → metrics → stories (2-4 days)</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/product-strategy-session">deanpeters/product-strategy-session</a></strong> - Full strategy session: positioning → framing → exploration → roadmap (2-4 weeks)</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/roadmap-planning">deanpeters/roadmap-planning</a></strong> - Strategic roadmap process: inputs → epics → prioritize → sequence → communicate (1-2 weeks)</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/deanpeters/Product-Manager-Skills/tree/main/skills/skill-authoring-workflow">deanpeters/skill-authoring-workflow</a></strong> - Meta workflow for authoring skills: choose path → validate → update docs → package</li>
</ul>
</details>

<summary><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Product Management Skills by Pawel Huryn</h3><a id="user-content-product-management-skills-by-pawel-huryn" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Product Management Skills by Pawel Huryn" href="#product-management-skills-by-pawel-huryn"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div></summary>
<p dir="auto">65 product management skills by <a href="https://github.com/phuryn">Paweł Huryn</a>, creator of The Product Compass newsletter. Covers the full PM lifecycle — from discovery and strategy to execution, analytics, and go-to-market — with frameworks from Teresa Torres, Geoffrey Moore, and more.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Data Analytics</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-data-analytics/skills/ab-test-analysis">phuryn/ab-test-analysis</a></strong> - Analyze A/B test results with statistical significance and recommendations</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-data-analytics/skills/cohort-analysis">phuryn/cohort-analysis</a></strong> - Cohort retention curves, feature adoption, and segment insights</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-data-analytics/skills/sql-queries">phuryn/sql-queries</a></strong> - Generate SQL queries from natural language across major dialects</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Execution</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/brainstorm-okrs">phuryn/brainstorm-okrs</a></strong> - Brainstorm team OKRs aligned with company objectives</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/create-prd">phuryn/create-prd</a></strong> - Create a PRD with 8-section template covering problem to release</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/dummy-dataset">phuryn/dummy-dataset</a></strong> - Generate realistic dummy datasets in CSV, JSON, or SQL</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/job-stories">phuryn/job-stories</a></strong> - Create job stories with acceptance criteria in JTBD format</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/outcome-roadmap">phuryn/outcome-roadmap</a></strong> - Transform output roadmaps into outcome-focused strategic plans</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/pre-mortem">phuryn/pre-mortem</a></strong> - Run pre-mortem risk analysis on PRDs and launch plans</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/prioritization-frameworks">phuryn/prioritization-frameworks</a></strong> - Reference guide to 9 prioritization frameworks with templates</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/release-notes">phuryn/release-notes</a></strong> - Generate user-facing release notes from tickets or changelogs</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/retro">phuryn/retro</a></strong> - Facilitate structured sprint retrospectives with action items</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/sprint-plan">phuryn/sprint-plan</a></strong> - Plan sprints with capacity, story selection, and risk mapping</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/stakeholder-map">phuryn/stakeholder-map</a></strong> - Build stakeholder maps with power/interest grid and comms plan</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/summarize-meeting">phuryn/summarize-meeting</a></strong> - Summarize meeting transcripts into structured notes and actions</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/test-scenarios">phuryn/test-scenarios</a></strong> - Create comprehensive test scenarios from user stories</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/user-stories">phuryn/user-stories</a></strong> - Create INVEST-compliant user stories with 3 C's structure</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-execution/skills/wwas">phuryn/wwas</a></strong> - Create backlog items in Why-What-Acceptance format</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Go-to-Market</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-go-to-market/skills/beachhead-segment">phuryn/beachhead-segment</a></strong> - Identify the first beachhead market segment for product launch</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-go-to-market/skills/competitive-battlecard">phuryn/competitive-battlecard</a></strong> - Create sales-ready battlecards against specific competitors</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-go-to-market/skills/growth-loops">phuryn/growth-loops</a></strong> - Identify growth loops across 5 flywheel types for traction</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-go-to-market/skills/gtm-motions">phuryn/gtm-motions</a></strong> - Identify best GTM motions across 7 types including PLG and ABM</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-go-to-market/skills/gtm-strategy">phuryn/gtm-strategy</a></strong> - Create GTM strategy with channels, messaging, and launch timeline</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-go-to-market/skills/ideal-customer-profile">phuryn/ideal-customer-profile</a></strong> - Identify ICP with demographics, behaviors, and JTBD</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Market Research</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-market-research/skills/competitor-analysis">phuryn/competitor-analysis</a></strong> - Analyze competitors with strengths, weaknesses, and differentiation</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-market-research/skills/customer-journey-map">phuryn/customer-journey-map</a></strong> - Map customer journeys with touchpoints, emotions, and opportunities</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-market-research/skills/market-segments">phuryn/market-segments</a></strong> - Identify 3-5 customer segments with JTBD and product fit</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-market-research/skills/market-sizing">phuryn/market-sizing</a></strong> - Estimate TAM, SAM, SOM with top-down and bottom-up approaches</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-market-research/skills/sentiment-analysis">phuryn/sentiment-analysis</a></strong> - Analyze user feedback with sentiment scores and JTBD insights</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-market-research/skills/user-personas">phuryn/user-personas</a></strong> - Create 3 user personas with JTBD, pains, and gains</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-market-research/skills/user-segmentation">phuryn/user-segmentation</a></strong> - Segment users by behavior, JTBD, and needs from feedback data</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Marketing &amp; Growth</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-marketing-growth/skills/marketing-ideas">phuryn/marketing-ideas</a></strong> - Generate 5 creative, cost-effective marketing ideas with rationale</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-marketing-growth/skills/north-star-metric">phuryn/north-star-metric</a></strong> - Define North Star Metric and input metrics constellation</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-marketing-growth/skills/positioning-ideas">phuryn/positioning-ideas</a></strong> - Brainstorm positioning ideas differentiated from competitors</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-marketing-growth/skills/product-name">phuryn/product-name</a></strong> - Brainstorm 5 memorable product names aligned to brand values</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://github.com/phuryn/pm-skills/tree/main/pm-marketing-growth/skills/value-prop-statements">phuryn/value-prop-statements</a></strong> - Generate value prop statements for marketing, sales, and onboarding</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Product Discovery</strong></p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔒 Security Notice</h2><a id="user-content--security-notice" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔒 Security Notice" href="#-security-notice"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Skills in this list are curated, not audited. They may be updated, modified, or replaced by their original maintainers at any time after being added here.</p>
<p dir="auto">Before installing or using any Agent Skill, review potential security risks and validate the source yourself.</p>
<p dir="auto">Recommended tools:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/snyk/agent-scan">Synk Skill Security Scanner</a></li>
<li><a href="https://ai.gendigital.com/agent-trust-hub" rel="nofollow">Agent Trust Hub</a></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Agent skills can include prompt injections, tool poisoning, hidden malware payloads, or unsafe data handling patterns. Always review the code and use skills at your own discretion.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Skill Quality Standards</h2><a id="user-content-skill-quality-standards" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Skill Quality Standards" href="#skill-quality-standards"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">As the ecosystem grows, consistent quality helps agents discover and use skills reliably. The following references and criteria keep the bar high.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quality Criteria</h3><a id="user-content-quality-criteria" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quality Criteria" href="#quality-criteria"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Area</th>
<th>Guideline</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Description</strong></td>
<td>Write in third person. State <em>what</em> the skill does and <em>when</em> to use it. Use specific keywords agents can match on (e.g., "PostgreSQL migration" not "database stuff").</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Progressive disclosure</strong></td>
<td>Keep top-level metadata under ~100 tokens. Skill body should stay below 500 lines. Load resources (large docs, schemas) on demand, not inline.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>No absolute paths</strong></td>
<td>Never hard-code machine-specific paths like <code>/Users/alice/</code>. Use relative paths or well-known variables (<code>$HOME</code>, <code>$PROJECT_ROOT</code>).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Scoped tools</strong></td>
<td>Request only the tools the skill actually needs. Avoid blanket <code>"tools": ["*"]</code>. Declare tool dependencies explicitly.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🤝 Contributing</h2><a id="user-content--contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🤝 Contributing" href="#-contributing"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We welcome contributions! See <a href="/VoltAgent/awesome-agent-skills/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md">CONTRIBUTING.md</a> for guidelines.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Submit new skills via PR</li>
<li>Improve existing definitions</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> Please don't submit skills you created 3 hours ago. We're now focusing on community-adopted skills, especially those published by development teams and proven in real-world usage. Quality over quantity.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributor <g-emoji class="g-emoji" alias="hearts">♥️</g-emoji> Thanks</h2><a id="user-content-contributor-️-thanks" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributor ♥️ Thanks" href="#contributor-️-thanks"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto">This is a curated list. Skills listed here are created and maintained by their respective authors and teams, not by us. We select community-adopted, proven skills and do not audit, endorse, or guarantee the security or correctness of listed projects. They are not security-audited and should be reviewed before production use.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you find an issue with a listed skill or want your skill removed, please <a href="https://github.com/VoltAgent/awesome-agent-skills/issues">open an issue</a> and we'll take care of it promptly.</p>
